Persona 5

100% completion, Persona 5 Royal 2022 Windows route · 18 areas · 940 tracked items

This page shows everything, including exact locations. It exists so you can search for one specific collectible and find it. If you are playing blind, use the interactive spoiler-free version instead, it hides locations until you ask for them, one item at a time.

Route and Save Safety

You are here when: Windows installation, title screen, content notice, and manual-save menu.

The selected PC route includes Royal and its bundled legacy content.

Chosen Windows Route

Route setup

HintUse the expanded PC edition.
WhereUse the expanded PC edition; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailInstall the October 2022 Windows release of Persona 5 Royal, update it, and verify that its bundled content is recognized before creating the completion file.

Bundled Content Archive

Route setup

HintThe PC edition includes a large legacy-content bundle.
WhereThe PC edition includes a large legacy-content bundle; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailOpen the content notice and confirm all forty-five bundled packs are registered. Record their costumes, Personas, accessories, challenge battles, music, and consumable boxes without relying on a storefront checklist.

First-Cycle Foundation

Route setup

HintBuild the calendar records before clear data.
WhereBuild the calendar records before clear data; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailUse the first file for all ordinary Confidants, requests, books, activities, equipment, Palace seed sets, Mementos stamps, outcome branches, and a nearly complete Persona registry.

New Game+ Foundation

Route setup

HintClear data opens the remaining optional records.
WhereClear data opens the remaining optional records; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailBegin from protected clear data, audit carried statistics and registry entries, then finish the plus-only fusion, optional encounters, alternate relationship branches, and any Thieves' Den awards still locked.

Five-Save Rotation (missable)

Route setup

HintProtect dates that control several records.
WhereProtect dates that control several records; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailKeep weekly, monthly, pre-deadline, mid-November, late-December, and early-February files. Never overwrite the four dated outcome branches.

Network-Independent Record

Route setup

HintOnline assistance is not completion evidence.
WhereOnline assistance is not completion evidence; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailDisable network features for one audit pass and verify that every finite guide record remains obtainable through local menus, fixed encounters, fusion, or calendar activities.

Difficulty Record

Route setup

HintChallenge settings are separate from finite content.
WhereChallenge settings are separate from finite content; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailComplete the chosen primary difficulty, record every custom parameter, and preserve a branch before the highest optional encounters so their clears can be demonstrated without erasing the main archive.

Carryover Audit

Route setup

HintClear data preserves only selected categories.
WhereClear data preserves only selected categories; verify it before scheduling April.
Full detailBefore New Game+, record social statistics, Compendium, money, equipment, skill cards, gifts, room decorations, challenge scores, and Thieves' Den state; then compare the new file and rebuild anything that reset.

Calendar Control

You are here when: School calendar, city map, bedroom, cafe, and evening activity menus.

A weekly ledger protects every finite social and activity window.

Matching Arcana Bonus

Calendar control

HintCarry a Persona matching the contact.
WhereCarry a Persona matching the contact; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailBefore a rank-building meeting, summon the matching Arcana, confirm the bonus notes, and reload if the expected rank does not advance.

Five Social Statistics

Calendar control

HintRaise every daily-life statistic steadily.
WhereRaise every daily-life statistic steadily; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailMax Knowledge, Guts, Proficiency, Kindness, and Charm through reading, study, jobs, crafting, meals, bathhouse visits, games, films, and scheduled events.

School-Day Priority

Calendar control

HintCampus contacts have the narrowest schedule.
WhereCampus contacts have the narrowest schedule; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailSpend ordinary school afternoons on campus-only Confidants first; use holidays and closures for town contacts, requests, and activities.

Weather and Seasonal Windows

Calendar control

HintRain and special dates alter several activities.
WhereRain and special dates alter several activities; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailBefore using a rainy or seasonal slot, check bathhouse bonuses, fishing conditions, shop exchanges, Palace access, Mementos weather, and available contacts.

Phone Message Retention (missable)

Calendar control

HintRead every request message promptly.
WhereRead every request message promptly; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailOpen new messages on arrival and record any target name immediately. The phone retains only fifty messages, so an ignored request lead can be lost.

Deadline Buffer (missable)

Calendar control

HintFinish active routes ahead of their final day.
WhereFinish active routes ahead of their final day; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailSecure each Palace route several days early, take every seed, make one Mementos sweep for unlocked requests, and keep the pre-deadline file until the following calendar audit.

Relationship Branch Archive (missable)

Calendar control

HintLate Confidant choices can split.
WhereLate Confidant choices can split; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailSave before every commitment choice, record both friendship and romance results locally, then continue on the branch that protects the planned all-romance or all-friendship award run.

Technical Rank

Calendar control

HintBilliards expands technical combinations.
WhereBilliards expands technical combinations; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailPlay billiards, obtain and read both specialist books when they unlock, and confirm Technical rank 4 in the status record.

Baton Pass Ranks

Calendar control

HintDarts develops party handoffs.
WhereDarts develops party handoffs; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailPlay darts with every eligible teammate until each Baton Pass rank reaches its cap, using a pre-evening save to avoid wasting sessions.

Weekly Audit

Calendar control

HintSmall calendar drift becomes permanent.
WhereSmall calendar drift becomes permanent; check it before spending the time slot.
Full detailAt each Monday save, compare Confidants, requests, books, games, seeds, equipment, registry percentage, activities, awards, and the next protected date.

Request 01, Beware the Clingy Ex-boyfriend

Mementos request

HintRead new phone messages and revisit contacts after calendar milestones.
WhereTo unlock it, check the phone on 5/7; its Mementos marker is on Qimranut 1.
Full detailRead and accept the phone lead before older messages displace it. Enter Mementos, descend to Qimranut 1, and open the marked encounter. Clear the marked encounter and remain until the request-complete notice appears. Confirm the reward entry named Attachment Pearl before leaving.

Palace Finite Records

You are here when: Temporary infiltration maps, safe rooms, grapple anchors, locked chests, and seed doors.

Clear every finite Palace object before its calendar window closes.

Every Palace Route (missable)

Palace record

HintTreat each temporary infiltration space as finite.
WhereTreat each temporary infiltration space as finite; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailSecure every route before its calendar deadline, open all mapped safe rooms, record every fixed chest and shortcut, and keep the pre-deadline save until the local audit is complete.

Every Will Seed Set (missable)

Palace record

HintEach active Palace hides a three-part set.
WhereEach active Palace hides a three-part set; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailCollect the red, green, and blue Will Seeds before leaving each Palace. Confirm the three pieces combine, then take the result to the Mementos trader for its upgraded form.

Grapple Routes

Palace record

HintRoyal adds high anchor paths.
WhereRoyal adds high anchor paths; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailInspect each highlighted grapple point, follow every optional upper route, and verify that no seed room, chest ledge, or shortcut remains shaded on the map.

Locked Chest Ledger (missable)

Palace record

HintCraft enough lockpicks before infiltration.
WhereCraft enough lockpicks before infiltration; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailOpen every fixed locked chest while the Palace remains available. Keep spare tools for Mementos and continue until the Thieves' Den locked-chest counter reaches forty.

Search-Object Ledger

Palace record

HintBreakable and searchable objects feed a finite award.
WhereBreakable and searchable objects feed a finite award; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailUse Third Eye on each route and collect at least 150 Palace search objects. Confirm the award counter, not inventory quantity, because materials can be spent.

Disaster Enemy Ledger

Palace record

HintUnstable enemies reward controlled chain damage.
WhereUnstable enemies reward controlled chain damage; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailTrigger Disaster explosions against nearby enemies until one hundred defeats are credited. Avoid finishing the unstable target before other enemies are inside the blast.

Ambush Ledger

Palace record

HintUse cover and grapple openings deliberately.
WhereUse cover and grapple openings deliberately; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailRecord fifty ordinary Palace ambushes and thirty grapple-initiated battles. Approach from concealment and verify advantage at the battle transition.

Escape Tool Ledger

Palace record

HintA detection recovery has its own finite record.
WhereA detection recovery has its own finite record; audit it from the current safe room.
Full detailAfter deliberate detection, use Smokescreen or Hypno Mist to escape combat five times. Confirm each evasion in the Thieves' Den counter before repeating.
